Potential plan for propose_candidate:
1. Load parent, maybe also generate from scratch for several lengths: uniform, random, parabolic, candidate shapes.

4. Use alternating sequence updates and local peak equalization.
5. Final high-beta log-softmax and coordinate descent.

We can implement `make_full(z)` top-level:
```
def make_full(z):
    n1 = len(z)
    full = np.empty(n1+1)
    m = z.mean()
    full[:-1] = z - m
    full[-1] = -z.sum()/n1
    return full
```
Need n1>0. For n=1 trivial.

```
def make_full(z): # z length n-1, last fixed to 0 in extended before centering
    s = z.sum()
    full = np.empty(n)
    full[:-1] = z - s/n
    full[-1] = -s/n
    return full
```

Now, LP best-response. We need implement safely. Use solve_lp_best_response(f, M, max_iters/time). f length n normalized sum1, M current max conv or target. Solve primal. Could use scipy.highs. Need avoid too large. We can build sparse banded A_ub for primal:
Rows k=0..2n-2, columns j range max(0,k-n+1)..min(k,n-1), value f[k-j]. This is convolution matrix. Build with `scipy.sparse.coo_matrix` or csr.
```
def linprog_best_response(f, M, timeout=None):
    n=len(f)
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
       js=np.arange(j0,j1+1)
       vals=f[k-js]
       rows.extend(np.full(len(js),k)); cols.extend(js); data.extend(vals)
    A=coo_matrix((data,(rows,cols)),shape=(2*n-1,n)).tocsr()
    c=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
    if res.status==0: return res.x
    return None
```

def fast_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    if n < 64: return np.convolve(a,a)
    M = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()? Need length for linear conv of a,a: 2n-1. next_power_of_two = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? If 2n-1 = e.g. 1023, (1023-1)=1022 bit_length=10 -> 1024? correct. Use `1 << (2*n - 1 - 1).bit_length()`? Simpler: `M = 1

- `soft_log_f(z, beta, maybe n)`. Need top-level no closures. It will use `make_full`.
```
def make_full(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    n1 = z.size
    n = n1+1
    s = z.sum()
    full = np.empty(n)
    full[:-1] = z - s/n
    full[-1] = -s/n
    return full
```
- `soft_log_value_grad(z, beta)`: 
```
full = make_full(z)
maxx = full.max()
b = np.exp(full - maxx)
S = b.sum()
if S <=0: return inf, zeros
b /= S
n = b.size
c = fast_conv(b)
cmax = c.max()
scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
scaled = np.clip(scaled, -100, 0) maybe min -700
exp = np.exp(scaled)
zsum = exp.sum()
L = cmax + np.log(zsum)/beta
p = exp/zsum
# H = 2*cross_corr(p,b)
Lfft = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
B = np.fft.rfft(b, Lfft)
P = np.fft.rfft(p, Lfft)
R = np.fft.irfft(B * np.conj(P), Lfft)[:n]
H = 2.0 * R
grad_full = 2.0*n*b*(H - 2.0*L)
grad_full -= grad_full.mean()
grad_z = grad_full[:-1]
return 2.0*n*L, grad_z
```

Value/grad function with mean gauge:

`fast_conv(a)`:
If n small use np.convolve. For large, use rfft. Need ensure a 1d float. For n=0? return empty.
```
def fast_conv(a):
 n=a.size
 if n==0: return np.empty(0)
 if n<128: return np.convolve(a,a)
 L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
 c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
 return c[:2*n-1]
```
Potential negative tiny; not issue. For n=1, L=1 <<0 =1, rfft ok, c length1.

`soft_log_f_mean(z,beta)`:
Need handle z length maybe 0. If z empty (n=1): value = 2? sequence [1], conv [1], score=2. Gradient [].
```
def soft_log_f_mean(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100, ...
    n1=z.size
    n=n1+1
    if n==1: return 2.0, np.zeros(0)
    full=make_full_mean(z)
    shift=full.max()
    # If shift huge? exp(full-shift)
    b=np.exp(full-shift)
    S=b.sum()
    if S <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    b=b/S
    c=fast_conv(b)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # ensure <=0
    scaled=np.minimum(0.0, scaled)
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    se=e.sum()
    if se<=0: se=1; e.fill? but max e=1 so se>=1
    Lc=cmax + float(np.log(se))/beta
    p=e/se
    # gradient H
    Lg=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()  # if n large maybe memory
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,Lg)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,Lg)
    R=np.fft.irfft(B*np.conj(P),Lg)[:n]
    H=2.0*R
    grad_full=2.0*n*b*(H - 2.0*Lc)
    if n1>0:
       grad_z = grad_full[:-1] - grad_full.sum()/n1
    else: zeros
    # clean nan
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)): grad_z=zeros
    val=2.0*n*Lc
    if not finite val: val=1e100
    return val, grad_z
```
Memory for Lg: n=16384 => 3n ~49150 nextpow65536, pfft length 32769 complex ~0.5MB? Actually 65536 rfft 32769*16=0.5MB, fine. b similarly. For n=65536 impossible; cap. We'll cap optimization n <=8192 or 16384. If n=16384, Lg=65536 (rfft length 32769) ~0.5MB, okay; FFT 65536 on 1 CPU maybe 0.2s per? Too slow. Cap 8192.

Potential bug: Gradient H for LSE wrt b uses p over c of length 2n-1. For beta high, p length. R = cross_corr p,b: `ifft(B * conj(P))` cyclic length Lg. Lg>=3n-2. Good. `make_full_mean(z)`:
```
def make_full_mean(z):
   z = np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
   n1=z.size; n=n1+1
   full=np.empty(n)
   if n1==0: full[0]=0
   else:
      s=z.sum()
      full[:-1]=z
      full[n-1] = -s/(n-1)
   return full
```
The full has sum 0. If z large, sum maybe large; okay.

`sequence_to_logvars(a)`:
```
def sequence_to_logvars(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   mx = a.max()
   if mx <=0: a=np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   floor = max(1e-300, mx*1e-18)
   ap = np.maximum(a, floor)
   x = np.log(ap)
   x -= x.mean()
   return x[:-1].copy()
```
If a has very small entries below floor, they get log floor + mean; okay.

`logvars_to_sequence(z)`:
```
def logvars_to_sequence(z):
   full=make_full_mean(z)
   shift=full.max()
   b=np.exp(full-shift)
   S=b.sum()
   if S<=0: return uniform
   return b/S
```

def run_log_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun=20000):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   if a.size<2: return score_sequence(a), a
   z0=sequence_to_logvars(a)

   z0=np.clip(z0, -60.0, 60.0)
   try:
      res = minimize(soft_log_f_mean, z0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter': max(100, maxfun//10), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-18, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxls': 50, 'disp': False})
      z=res.x if finite else z0
   except Exception:
      z=z0
   b=logvars_to_sequence(z)
   return score_sequence(b), b
```

Now, `fienup_square(a, deadline)`:
```
def fienup_square(a, deadline, max_iters=2000):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
   best_a=a; best_sc=score_sequence(a)
   if n<2: return best_a,best_sc
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
      C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
      Cmax=float(C.max()); Cmean=1.0/(2*n-1) # since sum C=1
      # target shape: maybe Cmean*(1+0.2*cos(2πk/(2n-1)))? Constant will boost edges too much? Conv edges are square of endpoints; if target constant, endpoints need sqrt(Cmean), maybe okay.
      alpha = 0.4 * ((max_iters-it)/max_iters)**1.5 + 0.05
      C_target = (1.0-alpha)*C + alpha*Cmean
      C_target = np.maximum(C_target, 1e-300)
      factor = np.sqrt(C_target / np.maximum(C, 1e-300))
      # To avoid huge factors, clip factor [0.5,2]? Maybe [0.2,5]
      factor = np.clip(factor, 0.2, 5.0)
      A_new = A * factor
      a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, L)[:n]
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
      S=a_new.sum()
      if S<1e-12: break
      a_new /= S
      sc=score_sequence(a_new)
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
   return best_a,best_sc
```
But if C is extremely small at edges, factor huge clipped 5; ok. This iterates flattening C. It might reduce score. Need maybe target not just mean but current lower envelope? For nonnegative C, mean constant. To reduce max, target maybe `np.minimum(C, Cmax*(1 - alpha))`? That only reduces peaks, not boost lows, causing sum of C no longer 1 (but update changes). We want conv sum automatically 1 after normalization? The DFT update with factor changes A, C sum may not be 1 before normalization; after a normalized, sum C=1. Target should integrate? If C_target sum not 1, factor scales DC? The DC of A? Factor at index 0 affects sum a; we normalize afterward, which rescales all A, changing C by xs^2. So integration okay. Fienup standard.

We can target a shape with slightly higher values at center? To reduce peak, target below max. Constant mean good. But if C has nonzero minimum, constant is above minimum and below max. Good.

Could also target `C_target = np.maximum(Cmean * (1 + eps * cos(2π*k/(2n-1))), 1e-12)` with eps small to respect support? Constant simpler.

       f = interpolate_sequence(f, max_n)

    # build diags

       res=None

...

Need `interpolate_sequence(a, new_n)`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return np.ones(new_n)
   if a.size==new_n: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out,0))
```
Could use periodic extension? Good.

Let's define top-level:
```
def cross_corr_pb(p, b, L=None):
   n=b.size
   if L is None: L=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()
   B=np.fft.rfft(b,L); P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   R=np.fft.irfft(B*np.conj(P),L)[:n]
   return 2.0*R
```
Then use in soft_log? To avoid code duplication. Top-level. But soft_log calls; okay.

Multiplicative function:
```
def multiplicative_peak_update(a, deadline, top_k=5, max_iters=2000):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_sc=score_sequence(a); n=a.size
    eta=0.15
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max()
       # top peaks
       k=min(top_k,c.size)
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:] if k>1 else ...
       idx=idx[c[idx].argsort()[::-1]]? Simpler use argsort for c size up to 8191, okay maybe slower but fine. Use np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k].
       w = c[idx]
       sw = w.sum()
       if sw<=0: break
       p=np.zeros(c.size); p[idx]=w/sw
       H = cross_corr_pb(p,a)
       L = float(np.dot(p,c))
       factor = H/(L+1e-300) - 2.0
       factor = np.clip(factor, -4.0, 4.0)
       a_new = a * np.exp(-eta*factor)
       S=a_new.sum(); if S<... break
       a_new/=S
       sc=score_sequence(a_new)
       if sc < best_sc: best...; if sc < current: a=a_new; eta=min(eta*1.05,0.5)
       else: eta*=0.7
    return best,best_sc
```
Could update less often. `np.argsort` per iter length 8192, 2000 => 16M comparisons, okay. Use argpartition for speed:
```
if k >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s) - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # load parent
    parent=None
    try:
       if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
          p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float).ravel()
          if p.size>=4:
             p=np.clip(p,0,1000); S=p.sum(); if S>1e-6: parent=p/S
    except: pass
    if parent is None:
       parent = make_shape(2048, 'uniform')? Use list.
    # evaluate parents
    sc=score_sequence(parent); update best.
    # create shapes for initial candidates
    n_initial = parent.size
    if n_initial > 8192: n_initial = 8192
    candidates = []
    for shape in ['uniform','sine','parabolic','cospeak','random_smooth']:
       a=make_shape(n_initial, shape, rng)
       update best.
    # sym of parent
    a=normalize_nonneg(0.5*(parent+parent[::-1])); update.
    # choose a_work: best if len<=4096 else interpolate to 4096? But best may be long. Keep separate.
    long_a = best_a if best_a.size <= 16384 else interpolate(best_a, 8192)
    work_a = best_a if best_a.size <= 4096 else interpolate_sequence(best_a, 4096)
    # Phase 1: Fienup on long_a (maybe parent long) for 8% time
    p1_end = t_start + 0.08*(budget_s-60)
    if long_a.size <= 16384 and time<p1_end:
       a,sc = fienup_square(long_a, p1_end, max_iters=5000)
       update.
       # also work from result downsample? if long improved
    # Phase 2: Log L-BFGS on work_a for 45%
    p2_end = t_start + 0.45*(budget_s-60)
    a_work = best_a if best_a.size <=4096 else interpolate(best_a,4096)
    #蛤 run schedule with betas maybe on current best work
    a_work, sc_work = adaptive_log_schedule(a_work, p2_end, ...)
    update.
    # after phase2, if work improved, maybe upsample to long length and Fienup? later.
    # Phase 3: LP iterations 15%
    p3_end = t_start + 0.60*(budget_s-60)
    lp_base = best_a if best_a.size <=2048 else interpolate(best_a, 1024?) # lp_iteration will downsample
    a_lp, sc_lp = lp_iteration(lp_base, p3_end, n_iters=30, max_n=1024)
    update.
    # If LP improved, maybe upsample to work length and log?
    if time<p3_end: a_lp2, sc2=lp_iteration(best, max_n=512) etc.
    # Phase 4: Multiplicative update and log on best (20%)
    p4_end = t_start + 0.80*(budget_s-60)
    a_mul, sc = multiplicative_peak_update(best_a if size<=4096 else interp 4096, p4_end, ...)
    update.
    # Run short log from best after multiplicative
    a_work = best if size<=4096 else interp 4096
    a_work, sc = adaptive_log_schedule(a_work, p4_end, high betas schedule?) # shorter
    update
    # Phase 5: Final Fienup on best (upsampled to original parent length if >4096) (10%)
    final_len = parent.size if parent.size <= 8192 else 8192
    final_a = best_a if best_a.size == final_len else interpolate(best_a, final_len)
    a_fin,sc = fienup_square(final_a, deadline, max_iters=5000)
    update.
    # Final multiplicative on final maybe 5%
    if time<deadline-30:
        a_fin,sc = multiplicative_peak_update(final_a, deadline, ...)
        update.
    # If still time, one more long log if final_len<=4096? else no.
    if final_len<=4096 and time<deadline-60:
        a_fin,sc = adaptive_log_schedule(final_a, deadline, betas_high, fraction small)
        update.
    # final verify and output
```

```
def adaptive_log_schedule(a, deadline, betas=None, alloc_seconds=None):
   if betas is None: betas=[...]; allocs=[...]
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best_a=a; best_sc=score_sequence(a)
   n=a.size
   # estimate eval time
   z=sequence_to_logvars(a)
   t0=time.monotonic(); val,grad=soft_log_f_mean(z, 100); t_eval=time.monotonic()-t0
   t_eval=max(1e-4, t_eval)
   for beta, sec in zip(betas, allocs):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
      rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
      sec_use = min(sec, 0.9*rem)
      if sec_use < 5: break
      maxfun = max(500, int(sec_use / (t_eval*1.7)))
      maxfun = min(maxfun, 100000) # cap to prevent runaway
      sc,b = run_log_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun)
      if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=b.copy()
      # Update a to b for warm start? If b improved, use b; else perhaps keep a? Use best_a as next start.
      a = best_a
      # If no improvement and beta high, perturb?
   return best_a, best_sc
```

Let's implement `spectral_flat_candidate(n)`:
```
def spectral_flat_candidate(n):
   L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
   C = np.ones(2*n-1)/(2*n-1)
   G = np.fft.rfft(C, L)
   # to avoid numerical phase issues, use sqrt of complex G
   A = np.sqrt(G.astype(np.complex128))
   a = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:n]
   a = np.maximum(a,0)
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

Can we improve by iterative deconvolution: Given a, compute C, target C_target = Cmean (or blend with current C to preserve support), compute a_new via sqrt(DFT C_target), clip, normalize. This is a fixed-point: 
```
C_target = (1-α) C + α * mean(C)
A = sqrt(fft(C_target))
a = clip(ifft(A))
```
This is different from Fienup square (which updates A by factor from C_target). It directly reconstructs a from desired C via spectral square root, not preserving phase. This may be powerful. Let's implement `spectral_target_iterate(a, n_iters, deadline, alpha)`:

- For it:

   C_target /= C_target.sum()

- This changes phase discontinuously; may converge to a fixed point of square-root operator. It directly targets a C shape. Could escape local minima. Implement `spectral_deconvolve_sequence(a, deadline, n_iters=5000)`:
```
def spectral_deconvolve_sequence(a, deadline):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best...
   n=a.size
   L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
   Cmean_len=2*n-1
   for it in range(n_iters):
      if time>deadline break
      progress=it/n_iters
      alpha = 0.1 + 0.9*(progress**1.5) # gradually
      C=fast_conv(a)
      Cmean = 1.0/(2*n-1)
      C_target = (1-alpha)*C + alpha*Cmean
      # maybe floor to avoid negative: C_target=np.maximum(C_target, 1e-15)
      C_target = C_target / C_target.sum()
      G = np.fft.rfft(C_target, L)
      # set tiny negative? G complex.
      A = np.sqrt(G.astype(np.complex128))
      a_new = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:n]
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
      S=a_new.sum()
      if S<1e-12: break
      a_new /= S
      sc=score_sequence(a_new)
      if sc<best: update
      # Maybe blend with old to avoid jumps: a = 0.7*a_new +0.3*a? The full reconstruction may be discontinuous. We can set a = a_new directly for target iteration, but score may worsen. To be safe, accept if improves? But if it worsens, target iteration may need to move through worse to improve. Keep a = a_new (or blend) to continue, but best tracks. Use a = normalize(0.5*a + 0.5*a_new) maybe.
      a = a_new # direct
   return best
```

Let's implement `spectral_deconvolve_sequence` with optional `direct=True`. Use time. To avoid getting stuck if a_new all zeros, fallback.

Pseudo:
```
def spectral_deconvolve_sequence(a, deadline, max_iters=10000, direct_mix=1.0):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_a=a; best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return
    L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    Cmean=1.0/(2*n-1)
    it=0
    while it<max_iters and time.monotonic()<deadline:
        progress = it/max(1,max_iters)
        alpha = 0.05 + 0.95*(progress ** 1.5)
        C=fast_conv(a)
        C=target? C_target=(1-alpha)*C + alpha*Cmean
        C_target=np.maximum(C_target, 1e-300)
        C_target/=C_target.sum()
        G=np.fft.rfft(C_target,L)
        A=np.sqrt(G.astype(np.complex128))
        a_new=np.fft.irfft(A,L)[:n]
        a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
        S=a_new.sum()
        if S<1e-12: break
        a_new/=S
        sc=score_sequence(a_new)
        if sc<best_sc: best...
        # blend with old for stability if direct_mix<1
        if direct_mix < 1:
            a_new = (1-direct_mix)*a + direct_mix*a_new
            a_new=normalize_nonneg(a_new)
        a=a_new
        it+=1
    return best_a,best_sc
```

`next_pow_len(N)`: if N<=1 return1 else `1 << (N-1).bit_length()`.

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    if n < 128:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    L=next_pow_len(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For n<128 np.convolve exact and fast. For n small, gradient H via np? We use FFT anyway. Good.

`fast_cross(a,b)`:
```
def fast_cross(a,b):
   a=...; b=...
   n=a.size; m=b.size
   if n==0 or m==0: return empty
   if n*m < 20000: return np.convolve(a,b)
   L=next_pow_len(n+m-1)
   return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,L)*np.fft.rfft(b,L),L)[:n+m-1]
```

`score_sequence`:
```
def score_sequence(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   c=fast_conv(a)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```

`cross_corr_pb(p,b,L=None)`:
```
def cross_corr_pb(p,b,L=None):
   n=b.size
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   if L is None: L=next_pow_len(3*n-2)
   B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   R=np.fft.irfft(B*np.conj(P),L)[:n]
   return 2.0*R
```
Need p length maybe; rfft pads. Good.

`soft_log_f_mean(z,beta)` as above. But in gradient if n=2, N-1=1, Lg=next_pow_len(4)=4. Good.

Need avoid repeated `fast_conv` for n small using np.convolve not vector? okay.

```
def make_full_mean(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    n1=z.size
    n=n1+1
    full=np.empty(n)
    if n1==0:
       full[0]=0.0
    else:
       full[:-1]=z
       full[n-1] = -float(z.sum())/n1
    return full
```

`sequence_to_logvars(a)`:
```
def sequence_to_logvars(a):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return np.zeros(0)
    mx=float(a.max())
    if mx<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n; mx=1.0/n
    floor = max(1e-300, mx*1e-18)
    ap = np.maximum(a, floor)
    x=np.log(ap)
    x -= x.mean()
    return x[:-1].copy()
```
Potential if a contains zeros and floor relative to max small; okay. For n large, mx~1/n; floor~1e-18/n. log diff ~41. Good.

`logvars_to_sequence(z)`:
```
def logvars_to_sequence(z):
   full=make_full_mean(np.asarray(z,float))
   if full.size==1: return np.array([1.0])
   shift=full.max()
   b=np.exp(full-shift)
   S=b.sum()
   if S<=0: return ones
   return b/S
```

`run_log_lbfgs(a,beta,maxfun)`:
```
def run_log_lbfgs(a,beta,maxfun=10000):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size<2: return score_sequence(a),a
    z0=sequence_to_logvars(a)
    maxfun=int(max(200,maxfun))
    try:
       res=minimize(soft_log_f_mean,z0,args=(beta,),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,options={'maxiter':max(50,maxfun//8),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50,'disp':False})
       z1=res.x
    except Exception as e:
       z1=z0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z1)): z1=z0
    b=logvars_to_sequence(z1)
    sc=score_sequence(b)
    return sc,b
```

def perturb_sequence(a, seed, sigma=0.05):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   noise=rng.standard_normal(n)*sigma
   a_new=a*np.exp(noise)  # preserves positive
   return normalize_nonneg(a_new)

def fienup_square(a, deadline, max_iters=10000):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   best_a=a; best_sc=score_sequence(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<2 or time.monotonic()>deadline: return best_a,best_sc
   L=next_pow_len(2*n-1)
   Cmean=1.0/(2*n-1)
   it=0
   while it<max_iters and time.monotonic()<deadline:
       progress=it/max(1,max_iters)
       alpha=0.25*(1-progress)**1.5 + 0.05
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
       C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
       C_target=(1-alpha)*C + alpha*Cmean
       C_target=np.maximum(C_target,1e-300)
       C_target/=C_target.sum()
       factor=np.sqrt(C_target/np.maximum(C,1e-300))
       factor=np.clip(factor,0.1,20.0)
       A_new=A*factor
       a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,L)[:n]
       a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
       S=a_new.sum()
       if S<1e-12: break
       a_new/=S
       sc=score_sequence(a_new)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
       # Accept even if worse? To follow the algorithm, set a=a_new. But if it worsens a lot, maybe blend.
       a_new_blend = 0.7*a + 0.3*a_new
       a = normalize_nonneg(a_new if sc<sm.current? )
```

```

`spectral_flat_candidate(n, use_unwrap=True)`:
```
def spectral_flat_candidate(n):
    if n<2: return np.ones(n)/n
    L=next_pow_len(2*n-1)
    C=np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
    G=np.fft.rfft(C,L)
    if use unwrap:
       mag=np.sqrt(np.abs(G))
       ang=np.angle(G)
       ang=np.unwrap(ang)
       # force ang[0]=0
       if G.size>0: ang = ang - ang[0] * np.arange? Wait unwrap already. Need if G[0] positive angle 0. But linear phase at k=0=0. Good.
       A=mag*np.exp(0.5j*ang)
       if L%2==0: A[-1]=np.real(A[-1])
    else:
       A=np.sqrt(G.astype(complex))
       if L%2==0: A[-1]=np.real(A[-1])
    a=np.fft.irfft(A,L)[:n]
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
    return a/S
```

The phase of box length odd: G_k = e^{-iπ k (2n-2)/L} * sine; angle = -π k(2n-2)/L mod π? For k=0..L/2, should be linear from 0 to -π? unwrap gives. Half gives -π k(n-1)/L, correct. Good.
If some G zero, angle 0 may cause jumps where magnitude zero; but mag zero, phase irrelevant. Good.
If A Nyquist imaginary due to angle near 2π? We force real. But if true Nyquist A should be negative real maybe; forcing real sign? Use `A[-1] = np.real(A[-1])` could zero if imaginary. But G_Nyquist positive for odd length, angle 0 or 2π; unwrap maybe 0, A real. good.

Now, `make_shape(n, kind, rng)`:
```
def make_shape(n, kind, rng=None):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if kind=='uniform': a=np.ones(n)
   elif 'sine': a=np.sin(np.pi*(x+1/(2*n))) + 1e-6
   elif 'parab': a=1-(2*x-1)**2
   elif 'cospeak': a=0.5*(1+np.cos(2*np.pi*x)); # could zero at ends? 1+cos zero at x=0.5 not ends.
   elif 'bell': a=np.exp(-((x-0.5)**2)/0.05)
   elif 'random': 
      if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
      a=np.exp(rng.standard_normal(n))*0.5 + 1.0
   else uniform
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

```
def lp_best_response(f, M=None, max_n=1024):
   if linprog is None or diags is None: return (None,None)
   f=np.asarray(f,float).ravel()
   if f.size==0: return None,None
   if f.size > max_n: f=interpolate_sequence(f,max_n)
   f=normalize_nonneg(f)
   n=f.size
   if n<4: return None,None
   if M is None:
      c=fast_conv(f); M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0: return None,None
   fs=f*n
   Ms=M*n
   try:
      offsets=-np.arange(n,dtype=int)
      diags_list=[]
      for d in range(n):
         diags_list.append(np.full(n-d, fs[d], dtype=np.float64))
      A=diags(diags_list, offsets, shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csr')
      c_vec=-np.ones(n)
      b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, Ms)
      # bounds: use [(0, None)]*n; for n large memory. maybe pass bounds=(0,None)? scipy linprog accepts bounds=(0,None) for all? Yes, bounds can be scalar? For linprog, `bounds` can be sequence or (min,max) tuple? It accepts bounds=(0,None) meaning same for all? I think it accepts tuple of length? Documentation: bounds can be sequence of (min,max) or a tuple (min,max) for all x. Use `bounds=(0.0, None)`. Good, avoid list.
      res=linprog(c_vec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   except Exception:
      try:
         A_dense=A.toarray()
         res=linprog(c_vec, A_ub=A_dense,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=(0.0,None),method='highs')
      except Exception:
         return (None,None)
   x=getattr(res,'x',None)
   if x is None: return (None,None)
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
   if x.size != n: return None,None
   x=np.maximum(x,0)
   B=float(x.sum())
   if B <= 1.0 + 1e-7: return (None,None)
   g=x/B
   # verify
   fg=fast_cross(f,g)
   if fg.size==0 or np.max(fg) > M*(1.0+1e-5) + 1e-12:
       return (None,None)
   return (g,B)
```

```
def multiplicative_peak_update(a, deadline, top_k=5):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best...; cur_sc=score; eta=0.15
   n=a.size; 
   for it in range(...):

      c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max()
      k=min(top_k,c.size)
      if k<c.size: idx=np.argpartition(c,-k)[-k:]; idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
      else: idx=np.arange(c.size)
      w=c[idx]; sw=w.sum(); if sw<=0 break
      p=np.zeros(c.size); p[idx]=w/sw

      L=float(np.dot(p,c))

         eta*=0.6
         if eta<0.005: break

def adaptive_log_schedule(a, deadline, betas=None, allocs=None, per_extra=2.5):
   ...

       alloc = allocs[idx] if idx<len else allocs[-1]

       if sec < 5: break

       run with last_beta
       # if no improvement for two chunks break? track

def sparse_H_from_peaks(b, idx, weights):
   n=b.size; H=np.zeros(n)
   for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
      k=int(k)
      if w<=0: continue
      i0=max(0, k-(n-1)); i1=min(k,n-1)
      if i1>=i0:
          # i from i0..i1, k-i ranges k-i0 .. k-i1 decreasing
          H[i0:i1+1] += 2.0*w*b[k-i0:k-i1+1][::-1]??

```

Implement `sparse_H_peaks(b, idx, weights)`:
```
def sparse_H_peaks(b, idx, weights):
    n=b.size; H=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
       if w <=0: continue
       k=int(k)
       i0 = max(0, k-(n-1))
       i1 = min(k, n-1)
       if i1 < i0: continue
       j0 = k - i1
       j1 = k - i0
       H[i0:i1+1] += (2.0*w) * b[j0:j1+1][::-1]
    return H
```
Check n=2,k=1: i0=0,i1=1,j0=0,j1=1 => H[0:2]+=2w*b[1:0:-1] = [b1,b0], H0=2w b1, H1=2w b0. Good.

```
def make_full_fixed(z):
    n1=z.size; n=n1+1; full=np.empty(n); full[0]=0.0; if n1: full[1:]=z; return full

Initialize z from a:
```
def sequence_to_logvars(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<2: return np.zeros(0)
   mx=a.max(); floor=max(1e-300,mx*1e-18); ap=np.maximum(a,floor)
   x=np.log(ap)
   x = x - x[0]
   return x[1:].copy()
```
If a[0] is zero, floor; first relative may be tiny; x[1:] large positive. okay.

full=make_full_fixed(z); shift=full.max(); b=np.exp(full-shift); b/=b.sum(); return b

`fienup_conv` alpha schedule: start small 0.05 to 0.5? Since target G_target scaled maybe. Use `alpha = 0.05 + 0.45 * (it/max_iters)`. It may converge. Factor clip 0.2,10.

Implementation:
```
def fienup_conv(a, deadline, max_iters=10000):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a); best...
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return
    L=next_pow_len(2*n-1)
    Cmean=1/(2*n-1)
    it=0
    while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       G=A*A
       C=np.fft.irfft(G,L)[:2*n-1]
       C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
       Cmax=float(C.max()); cur_sc=2*n*Cmax
       update current
       progress=it/max(1,max_iters)
       alpha = 0.05 + 0.5*(progress**1.0)
       C_target = (1-alpha)*C + alpha*Cmean
       C_target = np.maximum(C_target,0.0)
       cst=C_target.sum()
       if cst>0: C_target /= cst
       G_target=np.fft.rfft(C_target,L)
       den = G + (1e-300 + 0j)
       ratio = G_target / den
       factor = np.sqrt(ratio)
       mag = np.abs(factor)
       good = mag > 1e-12
       clip = np.clip(mag, 0.2, 10.0)
       factor2 = factor.copy()
       factor2[good] *= clip[good]/mag[good]
       # for mag tiny, factor remains maybe huge? set to 1
       factor2[~good] = 1.0
       A_new = A * factor2
       a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new,L)[:n]
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
       S=a_new.sum(); if S<... break
       a_new/=S
       a = a_new
       it+=1
    # final score
    c=fast_conv(a); sc=... update
    return best
```
The `factor2[good] *= clip[good] / mag[good]` works with boolean indexing. No closure. Good.

If alpha small, ratio close 1, good. If alpha 0.55, target half mean, could force. Good.

This Fienup targets exact C shape; for constant target, final A=constant target square root (phase maybe changes gradually). It may converge to a sequence whose C as flat as possible. Good.

Now, remove old fienup_square references. In propose, call `fienup_conv`.

`spectral_flat_candidate` remains. Good.

Now, `adaptive_log_schedule` still vital. 

Potential problem: Fienup_conv uses `G = A*A`; if A has large values, G overflow? a normalized, DFT max ~n maybe 4096, G ~1.6e7, fine.

Now, because C_target sum constant 1, G_target DC =1. Good.

Now, final code accordingly.

Let's update propose phase 1 variable names.

Final code draft (will write in final). Need ensure `fienup_conv` final score uses `score_sequence` or C. Use `score_sequence(a)`.

Now, if `fienup_conv` consumes phase, no separate score.

Now, think about score lower bound and target. Fienup to flat C with clipping may produce low. Good.

Let's consider initial `spectral_flat_candidate` with direct sqrt; if phase branch bad, Fienup from it may not fix. But we also have parent. Good.

Now, final output. Let's proceed to final. Ensure code fenced.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative step functions.

The objective is homogeneous, so all internal sequences are normalized to sum 1:
    score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a))

The program combines several complementary methods:
  - frequency-domain convolution flattening (Fienup-like, with A*A target),
  - log-domain L-BFGS with a smoothed max (soft LSE) objective,
  - sparse multiplicative peak-reduction updates,
  - a linear-programming best-response iteration,
  - spectral flat-convolution initialization.

The function propose_candidate(seed, budget_s) returns a list of floats.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import diags
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    diags = None


def next_pow_len(N):
    if N <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (N - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.5])
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / S


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 128:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    L = next_pow_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def fast_cross(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n * m < 20000:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    L = next_pow_len(n + m - 1)
    c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a, L) * np.fft.rfft(b, L), L)
    return c[:n + m - 1]


def score_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def make_full_fixed(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n1 = z.size
    n = n1 + 1
    full = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    full[0] = 0.0
    if n1:
        full[1:] = z
    return full


def cross_corr_pb(p, b, L=None):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow_len(3 * n - 2)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    R = np.fft.irfft(B * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
    return 2.0 * R


def soft_log_f_fixed(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n1 = z.size
    n = n1 + 1

    if n == 1:
        return 2.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n1, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.clip(z, -800.0, 800.0)
    full = make_full_fixed(z)
    shift = float(full.max())
    b = np.exp(full - shift)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n1, dtype=np.float64)
    b = b / S

    c = np.maximum(fast_conv(b), 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n1, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.minimum(0.0, scaled)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        e[0] = 1.0
        se = 1.0
    Lc = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta
    p = e / se

    Lg = next_pow_len(3 * n - 2)
    H = cross_corr_pb(p, b, Lg)
    grad_full = 2.0 * n * b * (H - 2.0 * Lc)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_full)):
        grad_full = np.zeros_like(grad_full)

    mean_g = float(np.mean(grad_full))
    grad_z = grad_full[1:] - mean_g

    val = 2.0 * n * Lc
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1.0e100
    return float(val), grad_z


def sequence_to_logvars(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(a.max())
    if mx <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        mx = 1.0 / n
    floor = max(1e-300, mx * 1e-18)
    ap = np.maximum(a, floor)
    x = np.log(ap)
    x = x - x[0]
    return x[1:].copy()


def logvars_to_sequence(z):
    full = make_full_fixed(np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if full.size == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    shift = float(full.max())
    b = np.exp(full - shift)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(full.size, dtype=np.float64) / full.size
    return b / S


def run_log_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun=5000, flip=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size < 2:
        return score_sequence(a), a

    work = a[::-1].copy() if flip else a.copy()
    z0 = sequence_to_logvars(work)
    maxfun = max(200, int(maxfun))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            soft_log_f_fixed,
            z0,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": max(50, maxfun // 8),
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-18,
                "gtol": 1e-12,
                "maxls": 50,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        z1 = res.x
    except Exception:
        z1 = z0

    z1 = np.asarray(z1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z1.size != z0.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(z1)):
        z1 = z0

    b = logvars_to_sequence(z1)
    if flip:
        b = b[::-1].copy()
    return score_sequence(b), b


def adaptive_log_schedule(a, deadline, betas=None, allocs=None):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    n = a.size

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    if betas is None:
        betas = [50.0, 150.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 30000.0, 100000.0, 300000.0]
    if allocs is None:
        allocs = [40.0, 60.0, 80.0, 100.0, 120.0, 150.0, 150.0, 120.0]

    z = sequence_to_logvars(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        soft_log_f_fixed(z, 100.0)
    except Exception:
        pass
    per_eval = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)

    flip = (n > 1 and a[-1] > 2.0 * a[0])

    no_improve = 0
    for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 15.0:
            break
        alloc = allocs[i] if i < len(allocs) else allocs[-1]
        sec = min(float(alloc), 0.60 * (deadline - now))
        if sec < 8.0:
            break
        cap = 30000 if n <= 2048 else 12000
        maxfun = int(max(300, sec / (per_eval * 4.0)))
        maxfun = min(maxfun, cap)
        sc, b = run_log_lbfgs(best_a, beta, maxfun=maxfun, flip=flip)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= 4:
            break

    last_beta = float(betas[-1])
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        sec = min(120.0, 0.80 * (deadline - now))
        if sec < 15.0:
            break
        cap = 20000 if n <= 2048 else 8000
        maxfun = int(max(300, sec / (per_eval * 4.0)))
        maxfun = min(maxfun, cap)
        sc, b = run_log_lbfgs(best_a, last_beta, maxfun=maxfun, flip=flip)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= 3:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def fienup_conv(a, deadline, max_iters=10000):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, score_sequence(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    L = next_pow_len(2 * n - 1)
    cmean = 1.0 / (2 * n - 1)

    it = 0
    while it < max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        G = A * A
        C = np.fft.irfft(G, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * C.max())
        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        progress = float(it) / float(max(1, max_iters))
        alpha = 0.05 + 0.55 * (progress ** 0.9)

        C_target = (1.0 - alpha) * C + alpha * cmean
        C_target = np.maximum(C_target, 0.0)
        s = float(C_target.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            break
        C_target = C_target / s

        G_target = np.fft.rfft(C_target, L)
        den = G + (1e-300 + 0.0j)
        ratio = G_target / den
        factor = np.sqrt(ratio)

        mag = np.abs(factor)
        good = mag > 1e-12
        clipped = np.clip(mag, 0.2, 10.0)
        factor2 = factor.copy()
        factor2[good] *= clipped[good] / mag[good]
        factor2[~good] = 1.0 + 0.0j

        A_new = A * factor2
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, L)[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        S = float(a_new.sum())
        if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
            break
        a = a_new / S
        it += 1

    final_sc = score_sequence(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def spectral_flat_candidate(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    L = next_pow_len(2 * n - 1)
    C = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    G = np.fft.rfft(C, L)
    A = np.sqrt(G.astype(np.complex128))
    if (L % 2) == 0:
        A[-1] = np.real(A[-1]) + 0.0j
    a = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:n]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a


def make_shape(n, kind, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n)
    elif kind == "sine":
        a = np.sin(np.pi * (x + 0.5 / n)) + 1e-6
    elif kind == "parab":
        a = 1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
    elif kind == "bell":
        a = np.exp(-((x - 0.5) ** 2) / 0.03)
    elif kind == "random":
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
        a = 0.6 + 0.4 * np.exp(0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    else:
        a = np.ones(n)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if a.size == new_n:
        return normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(b, 0.0))


def sparse_H_peaks(b, idx, weights):
    n = b.size
    H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, w in zip(idx, weights):
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        i0 = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        i1 = min(k, n - 1)
        if i1 < i0:
            continue
        j0 = k - i1
        j1 = k - i0
        H[i0:i1 + 1] += (2.0 * w) * b[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
    return H


def multiplicative_peak_update(a, deadline, top_k=5, max_iters=3000):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, score_sequence(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    eta = 0.15
    fail = 0

    it = 0
    while it < max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = np.maximum(fast_conv(a), 0.0)
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        k = min(int(top_k), c.size)
        if k <= 1:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        w = c[idx]
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw <= 0.0:
            break
        mask = w > 0.0
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        idx = idx[mask]
        w = w[mask] / sw

        H = sparse_H_peaks(a, idx, w)
        L = float(np.dot(w, c[idx]))
        if L <= 0.0:
            break

        factor = H / L - 2.0
        factor = np.clip(factor, -3.0, 3.0)
        a_new = a * np.exp(-eta * factor)
        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
        sc_new = score_sequence(a_new)

        if sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
        if sc_new < cur_sc:
            a = a_new
            fail = 0
            eta = min(0.60, eta * 1.03)
        else:
            fail += 1
            eta *= 0.65
            if eta < 0.004 or fail >= 25:
                break
        it += 1

    final_sc = score_sequence(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def lp_best_response(f, M=None, max_n=1024):
    if linprog is None or diags is None:
        return None, None

    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if f.size == 0:
        return None, None
    if f.size > max_n:
        f = interpolate_sequence(f, max_n)
    f = normalize_nonneg(f)
    n = f.size
    if n < 4:
        return None, None

    if M is None:
        M = float(np.max(fast_conv(f)))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None, None

    fs = f * float(n)
    Ms = M * float(n)

    A = None
    try:
        offsets = -np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        diag_list = []
        for d in range(n):
            diag_list.append(np.full(n - d, fs[d], dtype=np.float64))
        A = diags(diag_list, offsets, shape=(2 * n - 1, n), format="csr")

        c_vec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, Ms, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        res = linprog(
            c_vec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            if A is None:
                raise Exception("A unavailable")
            A_dense = A.toarray()
            c_vec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, Ms, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
            res = linprog(
                c_vec,
                A_ub=A_dense,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None, None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None, None
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return None, None
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    B = float(x.sum())
    if B <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
        return None, None

    g = x / B
    fg = fast_cross(f, g)
    if fg.size == 0:
        return None, None
    if np.max(fg) > M * (1.0 + 1e-5) + 1e-12:
        return None, None
    return g, B


def lp_iteration(f, deadline, max_n=1024, n_iters=20):
    f = interpolate_sequence(f, max_n)
    f = normalize_nonneg(f)
    best_a = f.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(f)
    cur_a = f.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(n_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        M = float(np.max(fast_conv(cur_a)))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            break
        g, B = lp_best_response(cur_a, max_n=max_n)
        if g is None:
            break

        scg = score_sequence(g)
        if scg < best_sc:
            best_sc = scg
            best_a = g.copy()

        t = 0.3
        if B > 1.5:
            t = 0.5
        if B > 2.5:
            t = 0.7

        cand = (1.0 - t) * cur_a + t * g
        cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
        sc = score_sequence(cand)

        if sc < cur_sc:
            cur_a = cand
            cur_sc = sc
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cur_a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 3:
                break
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    usable = max(10.0, total - 60.0)
    deadline = t_start + total - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    parent = None

    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                p = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(p.sum()) > 1e-9:
                    parent = p / float(p.sum())
                    best_a = parent.copy()
                    best_sc = score_sequence(parent)
                    print(f"[init] parent n={len(parent)} score={best_sc:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_shape(2048, "sine", rng)
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
        print(f"[init] no parent, sine n={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.8f}")

    base_n = int(best_a.size)
    if base_n < 512:
        base_n = 1024
    work_n = int(min(4096, max(1024, base_n)))

    def maybe_update_candidate(cand, label):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if cand is None:
            return
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
            return
        cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
        sc = score_sequence(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print(f"[update] {label}: n={len(cand)} score={sc:.8f}")

    maybe_update_candidate(interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n), "work-interp")
    maybe_update_candidate(spectral_flat_candidate(work_n), "spectral-flat")
    maybe_update_candidate(0.5 * (spectral_flat_candidate(work_n) + spectral_flat_candidate(work_n)[::-1]), "spectral-sym")
    for kind in ("sine", "parab", "bell", "random"):
        maybe_update_candidate(make_shape(work_n, kind, rng), f"shape-{kind}")

    work_best = interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n)

    # Phase 1: frequency-domain convolution flattening.
    p1_end = t_start + 0.12 * usable
    a1, sc1 = fienup_conv(work_best, p1_end, max_iters=6000)
    maybe_update_candidate(a1, "fienup-work")
    work_best = interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n)

    # Phase 2: log-domain smoothed-max optimization.
    p2_end = t_start + 0.52 * usable
    a2, sc2 = adaptive_log_schedule(work_best, p2_end)
    maybe_update_candidate(a2, "log-lbfgs-1")
    work_best = interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n)

    # Phase 3: LP best-response iterations.
    p3_end = t_start + 0.67 * usable
    if linprog is not None and diags is not None and time.monotonic() < p3_end - 30:
        lp_n = min(1024, max(512, work_n // 2))
        a3, sc3 = lp_iteration(interpolate_sequence(best_a, lp_n), p3_end, max_n=lp_n, n_iters=25)
        maybe_update_candidate(a3, "lp-1024")
        if time.monotonic() < p3_end - 30:
            a4, sc4 = lp_iteration(interpolate_sequence(best_a, 512), p3_end, max_n=512, n_iters=12)
            maybe_update_candidate(a4, "lp-512")

    # Phase 4: spectral restart + multiplicative peak reduction.
    p4_end = t_start + 0.82 * usable
    spect = spectral_flat_candidate(work_n)
    a5, sc5 = fienup_conv(spect, p4_end - 100.0, max_iters=4000)
    maybe_update_candidate(a5, "spectral-fienup")
    a6, sc6 = multiplicative_peak_update(interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n), p4_end, top_k=5, max_iters=1500)
    maybe_update_candidate(a6, "multiplicative-mid")

    # Another short log polish on the current work-scale best.
    p4b_end = min(p4_end + 0.04 * usable, deadline - 60.0)
    if p4b_end > time.monotonic() + 30:
        a7, sc7 = adaptive_log_schedule(
            interpolate_sequence(best_a, work_n),
            p4b_end,
            betas=[2000.0, 10000.0, 30000.0, 100000.0, 300000.0],
            allocs=[60.0, 90.0, 90.0, 90.0, 60.0],
        )
        maybe_update_candidate(a7, "log-lbfgs-2")

    # Phase 5: final polish at a longer length if the parent was longer.
    if parent is not None and parent.size > best_a.size and parent.size <= 8192:
        final_n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        final_n = max(int(best_a.size), work_n)
        final_n = int(min(final_n, 8192))

    final_a = interpolate_sequence(best_a, final_n)

    p5_end = deadline - 30.0
    if p5_end > time.monotonic() + 20:
        a8, sc8 = fienup_conv(final_a, p5_end, max_iters=8000)
        maybe_update_candidate(a8, "fienup-final")

    if p5_end > time.monotonic() + 20:
        a9, sc9 = multiplicative_peak_update(final_a, p5_end, top_k=5, max_iters=1000)
        maybe_update_candidate(a9, "multiplicative-final")

    if final_n <= 4096 and deadline > time.monotonic() + 60:
        a10, sc10 = adaptive_log_schedule(
            final_a,
            deadline - 15.0,
            betas=[10000.0, 30000.0, 100000.0, 300000.0],
            allocs=[80.0, 100.0, 80.0, 60.0],
        )
        maybe_update_candidate(a10, "log-lbfgs-final")

    out_a = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if out_a.size == 0 or float(out_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        out_a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in out_a]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print(f"FINAL verified={verified:.8f} internal={best_sc:.8f} n={len(out)}")
    except Exception:
        print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.8f} n={len(out)}")

    return out
```
